PP2A catalytic subunits are well-studied proteins where structural studies have provided key insights into their quaternary organization.<br><br>**Evaluating Options:**<br>- **A (Homodimer):** PP2A catalytic subunits are well-documented to form homodimers in crystal structures (e.g., PDB 3EMG shows human PP2A Cα as a homodimer).
